打開字典
	刺法論:	
又一法，於春分之日，日未出而吐之。又一法，於雨水日後，三浴以藥泄汗。又一法，小金丹方：辰砂二兩，水磨雄黃一兩，葉子雌黃一兩，紫金半兩，同入合中，外固了，地一尺築地實，不用爐，不須藥制，用火二十斤煆之也，七日終，候冷七日取，次日出合子，埋藥地中七日，取出順日研之三日，煉白沙蜜為丸，如梧桐子大，每日望東吸日華氣一口，冰水下一丸，和氣嚥之，服十粒，無疫干也。
		Another method is to perform it on the day of Vernal Equinox, before the sun rises, by exhaling. Another method is after the Rain Water solar term, to take three baths using medicinal herbs to induce perspiration and eliminate toxins. Another method is the Xiao Jin Dan formula: two liang of cinnabar, one liang of realgar ground with water, one liang of orpiment leaves, half a liang of purple gold. Place them all together in a sealed container and externally secure it firmly. Dig a hole one chi deep into the earth and compact the soil solidly; no furnace is needed, nor any additional medicinal processing. Use twenty jin of fire to calcine it for seven days. Afterward, wait another seven days until it cools completely before opening the container. On the next day, remove the medicine from its vessel and bury it in the earth for seven more days. Retrieve it, grind it finely over three days, then mix with refined white honey to form pills as large as Chinese 梧桐 (wutong) seeds. Each day, face east and take one breath of the sun's radiance, then swallow one pill with iced water while harmonizing your qi. Taking ten pills will prevent any epidemic from affecting you.

15
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	刺法論:	
黃帝問曰：人虛即神游失守位，使鬼神外干，是致夭亡，何以全真？願聞刺法。歧伯稽首再拜曰：昭乎哉問！謂神移失守，雖在其體，然不致死，或有邪干，故令夭壽。只如厥陰失守，天以虛，人氣肝虛，感天重虛，即魂游於上，邪干厥大氣，身溫猶可刺之，刺其足少陽之所過，次刺肝之俞。人病心虛，又遇君相二火司天失守，感而三虛，遇火不及，黑尸鬼犯之，令人暴亡，可刺手少陽之所過，復刺心俞。人脾病，又遇太陰司天失守，感而三虛，又遇土不及，青尸鬼邪犯之於人，令人暴亡，可刺足陽明之所過，復刺脾之俞。人肺病，遇陽明司天失守，感而三虛，又遇金不及，有赤尸鬼干人，令人暴亡，可刺手陽明之所過，復刺肺俞。人腎病，又遇太陽司天失守，感而三虛，又遇水運不及之年，有黃尸鬼干犯人正氣，吸人神魂，致暴亡，可刺足太陽之所過，刺足少陽之俞。
		The Yellow Emperor asked: "When a person is weakened, the spirit wanders and fails to maintain its proper position, allowing external influences from ghosts and spirits to invade. This leads to untimely death. How can one preserve true essence and remain whole?" I wish to hear about the acupuncture methods. Qibo bowed respectfully twice and replied: "What a clear question!" He said: "When the spirit shifts and fails to hold its proper position, although it remains within the body, this does not necessarily lead to death. However, if there is interference from external evils, it may result in an early demise." For example, if Jueyin fails to maintain its position, the heavens become deficient. If a person's Liver qi is also weak and they are affected by this double deficiency in heaven, their soul will wander upward, allowing external evils to interfere with major atmospheric energies. Even though the body remains warm, acupuncture can still be applied: first needle the Guo point of Foot-Shaoyan, then needle the Shu points o fliver meridian. When a person suffers from Heart deficiency and also encounters the failure of the sovereign and ministerial fire energies to maintain their heavenly positions, this results in triple deficiency. If they are further affected by insufficient Fire energy, the Black Corpse Ghost will invade them, causing sudden death. At such times acupuncture should be applied at the Guo point o fhand-Shaoyang meridian, followed by needling of Heart Shu points. When a person suffers from Spleen illness and also encounters the failure of Taiyin to maintain its heavenly position, this results in triple deficiency; if they are further affected by insufficiency of Earth energy, the Green Corpse Ghost will attack them, causing sudden death. In such cases acupuncture should be applied at the Gūo point of Foot-Yangming meridian, followed by needle treatment at Spleen Shu points. When a person suffers from Lung illness and encounters the failure of Yangming to maintain its heavenly position, triple deficiency results; if they are further afflicted by insufficiency in Metal energy, the Red Corpse Ghost will interfere with them, causing sudden death. Acupuncture should be applied at the Guò point of Hand-Yangming meridian followed by treatment at Lung Shu points. When a person suffers from Kidney illness and also encounters the failure o ftaiyang to maintain its heavenly position, it results in triple deficiency. When they further encounter years of insufficient Water movement, the Yellow Corpse Ghost will violate their true qi, drawing away their spirit and soul, leading to sudden death. At this time acupuncture should be applied at Guò point of Foot-Taiyang meridian, then needle treatment should be given at Shu points o ffoot-Shaoyang meridians.

16
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	刺法論:	
黃帝問曰：十二藏之相使，神失位，使神彩之不圓，恐邪干犯，治之可刺，願聞其要。歧伯稽首再拜曰：悉乎哉，問至理，道真宗，此非聖帝，焉究斯源，是謂氣神合道，契符上天。心者，君主之官，神明出焉，可刺手少陰之源。肺者，相傳之官，治節出焉，可刺手太陰之源。肝者，將軍之官，謀慮出焉，可刺足厥陰之源。膽者，中正之官，決斷出焉，可刺足少陽之源。膻中者，臣使之官，喜樂出焉，可刺心包胳所流。脾為諫議之官，知周出焉，可刺脾之源。胃為倉廩之官，五味出焉，可刺胃之源。大腸者，傳道之官，變化出焉，可刺大腸之源。小腸者，受盛之官，化物出焉，可刺小腸之源。腎者，作強之官，伎巧出焉，刺其腎之源。三焦者，決瀆之官，水道出焉，刺三焦之源。膀胱者，州都之官，精液藏焉，氣化則能出矣，刺膀胱之源。凡此十二官者，不得相失也。是故刺法有全神養真之旨，亦法有修真之道，非治疾也，故要修養和神也。道貴常存，補神固根，精氣不散，神守不分，然即神守而雖不去，亦全真，人神不守，非達至真，至真之要，在乎天玄，神守天息，復入本元，命曰歸宗。
		The Yellow Emperor asked: "Regarding the twelve zang organs and their mutual functions, when the spirit fails to maintain its proper position it causes the brilliance of the spirit to become incomplete. I fear that this may invite external evils to invade. If treatment is needed through acupuncture, I would like to hear the essentials." Qibo bowed respectfully twice and answered: "What a thorough question! You are inquiring about the ultimate principles and the true essence of the Dao. Without being a sage emperor, who could ever trace back to such origins? This is called harmonizing qi and spirit with the Dao, aligning with the will of heaven above." The Heart is the official of sovereign authority; from it arises the spirit and clarity. One may needle at the Yuan point of Hand-Shaoyin. The Lungs are the official of transmission; from them arises regulation and control. One may needle at the Yuán point of Hand-Taiyín meridian. The Liver is the official of the general; from it arise strategy and deliberation. One may needle at the Yua point of Foot-Jueyn meridians. The Gallbladder is the official of impartial judgment; from it arise decisiveness and resolution. One may needle at its Yuan point on the Foot-Shaoyang meridian. The Zhongshu is the official of ministerial service; from it arise joy and happiness. One may needle at the Liu point o fheart-Pericardium meridians. The Spleen is the official of advisory counsel; from it arises awareness and circumspection. One may needle at its Yuán point. The Stomach is the official of granaries; from it arise the five flavors. One may needle at its Yua point. The Large Intestine is the official of transmission and passage; from it arise transformation and change. One may needle at its source point. The Small Intestine is the official o f receiving and holding; from it arises transformation of substances. One may needle at its yuan point. The Kidneys are the official o f strength and vigor; from them arise skill and dexterity. One should needle at their source point. The Triple Burner is the official of waterways; from it arises the passage o f fluids. One should needle at its source point. The Bladder is the official o f the region's capital; it stores essence and fluids, and through qi transformation they are able to be expelled. One should needle at its Yua point. All these twelve officials must not lose their proper functions or coordination. Therefore, the method of acupuncture contains the principle o f preserving the spirit and nurturing true essence. There is also a way to cultivate one's true nature, which is not merely for treating diseases; thus, it is essential to nurture harmony and preserve the spirit. The Dao values constant preservation; to nourish the spirit and consolidate its roots, so that essence and qi do not disperse, and the spirit remains undivided. Thus, when the spirit is safeguarded and does not depart, true essence is preserved in full. If a person's spirit fails to remain guarded, they have not attained ultimate truth. The key to ultimate truth lies in heaven's mystery; when the spirit guards heavenly breath, it returns to its original source—this is called returning to one's origin. 

73. 本病論 On Internal Diseases

1
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
黃帝問曰：天元九窒，余已知之，願聞氣交，何名失守？歧伯曰：謂其上下升降，遷正退位，各有經論，上下各有不前，故名失守也。是故氣交失易位，氣交迺變，變易非常，即四時失序，萬化不安，變民病也。
		The Yellow Emperor asked: "I have already learned about the Nine Obstructions of Tianyuan, and I wish to hear about the Interchange of Qi. What is meant by 'loss of defense'?" Qibo said: "This refers to the ascending and descending movements between upper and lower levels. When there is a shift in the proper position or retreat from it, each has its own canonical explanation. If either the upper or lower movement fails to proceed, this is called 'loss of defense.'" Therefore, when the Interchange of Qi fails and positions are improperly exchanged, the Interchange itself will change. When such changes occur frequently and abnormally, the four seasons lose their order, all transformations become unstable, and this leads to illness among the people.

2
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
帝曰：升降不前，願聞其故，氣交有變，何以明知？
		The Emperor asked: "When ascending and descending movements do not proceed, I wish to learn the reason behind it. When there is a change in the Interchange of Qi, how can one clearly know about it?"

3
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
歧伯曰：昭乎問哉！明乎道矣。氣交有變，是謂天地機，但欲降而不得降者，地窒刑之。又有五運太過，而先天而至者，即交不前，但欲升而不得其升，中運抑之，但欲降而不得其降，中運抑之。於是有升之不前，降之不下者，升而至天者，有升降俱不前，作如此之分別，即氣交之變，變之有異，常各各不同，災有微甚者也。
		Qibo said: "What an insightful question!" You have indeed understood the principle. When there is a change in the Interexchange of Qi, it is called the mechanism of Heaven and Earth. If something wishes to descend but cannot do so, it is obstructed by the earth's constraint. There are also cases where the Five Periodic Cycles are excessive, arriving before their time. In such situations, the Interchange does not proceed forward; if something wishes to ascend but cannot do so, or wishes to descend yet fails to do so, it is restrained by the central cycle. Thus, there are cases where ascending does not proceed and descending does not occur. When something ascends to the heavens, or when both ascending and descending fail to proceed, such distinctions must be made. These represent changes in the Interchange of Qi; these changes vary greatly, each being different from another, resulting in disasters that range from mild to severe.

4
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
帝曰：願聞氣交遇會勝抑之由，變成民病，輕重何如？歧伯曰：勝相會，抑伏使然。是故辰戌之歲，木氣升之，主逢天柱，勝而不前。又遇庚戌，金運先天，中運勝之，忽然不前。木運升天，金迺抑之，升而不前，即清生風少，肅殺於春，露霜復降，草木乃萎。民病溫疫早發，咽嗌迺乾，四肢滿，肢節皆痛。久而化鬱，即大風摧拉，折隕鳴紊。民病卒中徧痹，手足不仁。
		The Emperor asked: "I wish to hear about the causes of interactions and suppressions when the Interchange of Qi occurs, how these changes lead to illness among the people, and what are their degrees of severity?" Qibo said: "When a dominant force meets another, suppression results from this interaction." Therefore, in the years of Chen and Xu, the Wood Qi rises, encountering Tianzhu as its host. It is dominant yet fails to proceed forward. Additionally, when it encounters Geng Xu, the Metal Cycle arrives before its time; the central cycle dominates it, and suddenly it cannot proceed forward. When the Wood Cycle ascends to Heaven, it is suppressed by Metal. Although it attempts to ascend, it does not proceed forward. As a result, cold and dry winds arise prematurely in spring;肃杀 (severe killing) occurs, dew and frost fall again, and grasses and trees wither. The people suffer from early outbreaks of warm epidemics; their throats become dry, their limbs feel full, and all joints experience pain. After a long time, this stagnation transforms into heat; then strong winds arise suddenly, causing destruction, breaking things apart, and creating chaotic sounds. The people suffer from sudden strokes and generalized numbness, with their hands and feet becoming unresponsive.

5
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故己亥之歲，君火升天，主窒天蓬，勝之不前。又厥陰木遷正，則少陰未得升天，水運以至其中者。君火欲升，而中水運抑之，升之不前，即清寒復作，冷生旦暮。民病伏陽，而內生煩熱，心神驚悸，寒熱閒作。日久成鬱，即暴熱迺至，赤風腫翳，化疫，溫癘暖作，赤氣瘴而化火疫，皆煩而躁渴，渴甚治之以泄之可止。
		Therefore, in the year Ji Hai, the Sovereign Fire ascends to Heaven; it encounters Tianpeng as its host and is obstructed from proceeding forward. Furthermore, when Jueyin Wood shifts to the proper position, Shaoyin has not yet ascended to Heaven; instead, Water Cycle arrives in its place. The Sovereign Fire wishes to ascend but is suppressed by the central Water Cycle, so it cannot proceed forward. As a result, cool and cold conditions arise again, with chilliness occurring at dawn and dusk. The people suffer from suppressed Yang internally, which generates internal heat and restlessness; their minds become startled and agitated, with alternating chills and fevers. If this condition persists for a long time, it becomes stagnated heat; then sudden intense heat arises. Red winds and swollen 翳 (obscuration) appear, transforming into epidemics. Warm fevers and warm plagues emerge, with red Qi forming miasma that transforms into fire-related epidemics. People become restless, agitated, and thirsty. When thirst is severe, treatment by purgation can stop the condition.

6
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故子午之歲，太陰升天，主窒天沖，勝之不前。又或遇壬子，木運先天而至者，中木遇抑之也。升天不前，即風埃四起，時舉埃昏，雨溼不化。民病風厥涎潮，徧痹不隨，脹滿。久而伏鬱，即黃埃化疫也，民病夭亡，臉肢府黃疸滿閉，溼令弗布，雨化迺微。
		Therefore, in the years Zi Wu, Taiyin ascends to Heaven and encounters Tianchong as its host; it is obstructed from proceeding forward due to dominance. Additionally, if Ren Zi occurs, the Wood Cycle arrives before its time, and it is suppressed by central Wood forces. The ascent to Heaven does not proceed; thus, windborne dust arises everywhere, causing frequent haze and dimness, with rain and dampness failing to dissipate. The people suffer from wind-induced syncope and excessive saliva flow; generalized numbness occurs, limbs do not move properly, and distension and fullness arise. After a long time, the stagnation becomes severe; yellow dust transforms into an epidemic. The people suffer from premature death and jaundice affecting their faces, limbs, and internal organs, with obstruction and fullness occurring. Damp conditions fail to disperse properly, and rainfall diminishes slightly.

7
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故丑未之年，少陽升天，主窒天蓬，勝之不前。又或遇太陰未遷正者，即少陰未升天也，水運以至者。升天不前，即寒雰反布，凜冽如冬，水復涸，冰再結，暄暖乍作，冷復布之，寒暄不時。民病伏陽在內，煩熱生中，心神驚駭，寒熱閒爭。以久成鬱，即暴熱迺生，赤風氣瞳翳，化成鬱癘，迺作伏熱內煩，痹而生厥，甚則血溢。
		Therefore, in the years Chou Wei, Shaoyang ascends to Heaven but encounters Tianpeng as its host; it is dominant yet fails to proceed. Additionally, if Taiyin has not yet shifted into the proper position, then Shaoyin also fails to ascend to Heaven; in this case, the Water Cycle arrives instead. The ascent to Heaven does not proceed, so cold mists spread again; the chill is as severe as winter. Water dries up once more, ice forms again, and brief warmth appears suddenly, only for cold conditions to return. Cold and warm weather occur at irregular times. The people suffer from internal suppressed Yang, which generates heat within; their minds become startled and frightened, with alternating chills and fever. If this condition persists, it becomes stagnated and generates sudden intense heat. Red winds cause eye obscurations; this transforms into a stagnant plague, resulting in internal heat 烦 (restlessness) and numbness leading to syncope. In severe cases, blood may overflow.

8
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故寅申之年，陽明升天，主窒天英，勝之不前。又或遇戊戊寅，火運先天而至。金欲升天，火運抑之，升之不前，即時雨不降，西風數舉，鹹鹵燥生。民病上熱喘嗽血溢。久而化鬱，即白埃翳霧，清生殺氣，民病脅滿悲傷，寒鼽嚏嗌乾，手拆皮膚燥。
		Therefore, in the years Yin Shen, Yangming ascends to Heaven but meets Tianying as its host; it dominates yet fails to proceed forward. Additionally, if one encounters Wu Yin, the Fire Cycle arrives before its time. Metal wishes to ascend to Heaven, but the Fire Cycle suppresses it; thus, its ascent does not proceed. As a result, timely rains do not fall, western winds frequently arise, and salty alkali conditions become dry and arid. The people suffer from upper-body heat, coughing with shortness of breath, and blood overflow. After a long time, stagnation transforms further; white dust and obscurations form fog, with cold and killing Qi arising. The people suffer from fullness in the costal region, sadness, chills, sneezing, dry throat, cracked hands, and dry skin.

9
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故卯酉之年，太陽升天，土窒天內，勝之不前。又遇陽明未遷正者，即太陽未升天也，土運以至。水欲升天，土運抑之，升之不前，即溼而熱蒸，寒生雨閒。民病注不，食不及化。久而成鬱，冷來客熱，冰雹卒至。民病厥逆而噦，熱生於內，氣痹於外，足脛痠疼，反生心悸懊熱，暴煩而復厥。
		Therefore, in the years Mao You, Taiyang ascends to Heaven but is obstructed by Earth within Tiannei; it dominates yet does not proceed forward. Additionally, if Yangming has not yet shifted to the proper position, then Taiyang also fails to ascend to Heaven, and instead the Earth Cycle arrives. Water wishes to ascend to Heaven but is suppressed by the Earth Cycle, so its ascent does not proceed; as a result, dampness and heat rise together, with cold arising between rains. The people suffer from retention of food, unable to digest their meals properly. After a long time, cold gives rise to external heat; hail suddenly falls. The people suffer from reversal of Qi and vomiting, with heat arising internally; Qi becomes obstructed externally. Their calves become sore and painful, while they experience palpitations, restlessness, internal heat, sudden irritability, and recurring syncope.

10
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
黃帝曰：升之不前，余已盡知其旨。願聞降不下，可得明乎？歧伯曰：悉乎哉問！是之謂天地微旨，可以盡陳斯道，所謂升已降也。至天三年，次歲必降，降而入地，始為左閒也。如此升降往來，命之六紀者矣。是故丑未之歲，厥陰降地，主窒地皛，勝而不前。又或遇少陰未退位，即厥陰未降下，金運以至中。金運承之，降之不下，抑之變鬱，木欲降下，金承之，降而不下，蒼埃遠見，白氣承之，風舉埃昏，清躁行殺，霜露復下，肅殺布令。久而不降，抑之化鬱，即作風躁相伏，暄而反清，草木萌動，殺霜乃，蟄未見，懼清傷藏。
		The Yellow Emperor said: "I have now fully understood the meaning of failure to ascend." I wish to hear about failure to descend. May I know it clearly? Qibo said: "What a thorough question!" This is what is meant by the subtle principles of Heaven and Earth; it can fully explain this principle, which is referred to as ascending followed by descending. After reaching the heavens for three years, in the following year it must descend; when it descends into Earth, this marks the beginning of the left interval. In this way, ascending and descending movements proceed back and forth; this is what is called the Six Cycles. Therefore, in the years Chou and Wei, Jueyin descends to Earth but encounters Dikao as its host; it is dominant but does not proceed forward. Additionally, if Shaoyin has not yet vacated its position, then Jueyin also does not descend; instead, the Metal Cycle arrives in the middle. The Metal Cycle supports it, yet the descent does not occur; suppression leads to stagnation. Wood wishes to descend, but is supported by Metal; thus, it descends without success. Blue dust appears at a distance, white Qi follows it, wind raises dust and obscuration, cold dryness executes killing power, frost and dew fall again, and severe killing commands are issued. If the descent is prolonged and suppression leads to stagnation, then wind and dryness will become latent; warmth appears but turns cold again. Grasses and trees begin to sprout, yet killing frosts occur, burrowing creatures do not emerge, and there is fear of cold damaging storage functions.

11
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故寅申之歲，少陰降地，主窒地玄，勝之不入。又或遇丙申丙寅，水運太過，先天而至。君火欲降，水運承之，降而不下，即彤雲纔見，黑氣反生，暄暖如舒，寒常布雪，凜冽復作，天雲慘悽。久而不降，伏之化鬱，寒勝復熱，赤風化疫，民病面赤心煩，頭痛目眩也，赤氣彰而溫病欲作也。
		Therefore, in the years Yin and Shen, Shaoyin descends to Earth but meets Dixuan as its host; it is dominant and yet does not enter. Additionally, if one encounters Bing Shen or Bing Yin, the Water Cycle is excessive and arrives before its time. The Sovereign Fire wishes to descend, yet is supported by the Water Cycle; thus, it descends but does not reach Earth. As a result, red clouds appear briefly, black Qi arises instead, warmth spreads as if relaxed, cold snow frequently falls, and severe chill returns, with darkened skies appearing gloomy and sorrowful. If the descent remains prolonged, latent stagnation forms; cold dominates and heat arises in response. Red winds transform into epidemics, causing people to suffer from red faces, mental restlessness, headaches, and dizziness. The red Qi becomes evident, indicating that warm diseases are about to arise.

12
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故卯酉之歲，太陰降地，主窒地蒼，勝之不入。又或少陽未退位者，即太陰未得降也，或木運以至。木運承之，降而不下，即黃雲見而青霞彰，鬱蒸作而大風，霧翳埃勝，折損迺作。久而不降也，伏之化鬱，天埃黃氣，地布溼蒸，民病四肢不舉，昏眩肢節痛，腹滿填臆。
		Therefore, in the years Mao and You, Taiyin descends to Earth yet meets Dicang as its host; it is dominant, but does not enter. Additionally, if Shaoyang has not yet vacated its post, then Taiyin cannot descend; in this case, perhaps the Wood Cycle arrives. The Wood Cycle supports it, but the descent does not proceed; yellow clouds appear and green halos become evident. Stagnant heat rises, causing strong winds; fog and obscurations dominate, leading to breaking and damage. If the descent remains long and does not proceed, latent stagnation forms. Yellow dust and Qi appear in the sky; damp heat spreads on Earth. The people suffer from inability to move their limbs, dizziness, joint pain, abdominal fullness, and chest congestion.

13
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故辰戊之歲，少陽降地，主窒地玄，勝之不入。又或遇水運太過，先天而至也。水運承之，水降不下，即彤雲纔見，黑氣反生，暄暖欲生，冷氣卒至，甚即冰雹也。久而不降，伏之化鬱，冷氣復熱，赤風化疫，民病面赤心煩，頭痛目眩也，赤氣彰而熱病欲作也。
		Therefore, in the years Chen Wu, Shaoyang descends to Earth but is obstructed by Dixuan as its host; although dominant, it does not enter. Additionally, if the Water Cycle is excessive and has arrived before its time. The Water Cycle supports it, yet water fails to descend; thus, red clouds appear briefly while black Qi arises instead. Warmth is about to emerge, but cold air suddenly arrives; in severe cases, hail forms. If the descent remains prolonged and latent stagnation forms, cold Qi gives rise to heat; red winds transform into epidemics. The people suffer from flushed faces, mental restlessness, headache, dizziness; the red Qi becomes evident and indicates that hot diseases are about to occur.

14
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故己亥之歲，陽明降地，主窒地彤，勝而不入。又或遇太陰未退位，即少陽未得降，即火運以至之。火運承之不下，即天清而肅，赤氣迺彰，暄熱反作。民皆旨倦，夜臥不安，咽乾引飲，懊熱內煩，大清朝暮，暄還復作，久而不降，伏之化鬱，天清薄寒，遠生白氣。民病掉眩，手足直而不仁，兩脅作痛，滿目忙忙。
		Therefore, in the year Ji Hai (and presumably also for other years with similar configurations), Yangming descends to Earth and encounters Diting as its host; it is dominantyet does not enter. Additionally, if Taiyin has yet to vacate its position, then Shaoyang cannot descend; in such a case, the Fire Cycle arrives. The Fire Cycle supports it but does not allow descent; thus, the sky becomes clear and severe, red Qi becomes evident, and warmth with heat arises unexpectedly. The people feel weary and exhausted, unable to rest at night; their throats become dry and they crave drinks, suffering from internal heat and irritability. In the early morning, it is cool and clear, but warmth returns again in the evening. If this condition persists without descent, latent stagnation forms: the sky remains clear with slight coldness, while white Qi appears at a distance. The people suffer from dizziness and vertigo; their hands and feet become stiff and unresponsive, with pain in both costal regions and a sensation of visual confusion.

打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
是故子午之年，太陽降地，主窒地阜勝之，降而不入。又或遇土運太過，先天而至。土運承之，降而不入，即天彰黑氣，暝暗悽慘，纔施黃埃而布溼，寒化令氣，蒸溼復令。久而不降，伏之化鬱，民病大厥，四肢重怠，陰痿少力，天布沈陰，蒸溼閒作。
		Therefore, in the years Zi and Wu, Taiyang descends to Earth but is hindered by Difu as its host; it prevails yet does not enter. Additionally, if one encounters an excessive Earth Cycle that arrives before its time. The Earth Cycle supports it, yet descent does not occur; thus, black Qi appears in the sky, darkness and gloom prevail. Yellow dust is barely dispersed before dampness spreads again, cold transforms into commanding Qi, and steamy dampness returns to dominate. If the descent remains prolonged without occurring, latent stagnation forms and transforms further; the people suffer from severe syncope, heavy and weak limbs, weakness in Yin functions with diminished strength. The sky is overcast with deep gloom, while steamy dampness alternates in occurrence.

16
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
帝曰：升降不前，晰如其宗，願聞遷正，可得明乎？歧伯曰：正司中位，是謂遷正位，司天不得其遷正者，即前司天以過交司之。即遇司天太過有餘日也，即仍舊治天數，新司天未得遷正也。厥陰不遷正，即風暄不時，花卉萎瘁，民病淋溲，目系轉，轉筋喜怒，小便赤。風欲令而寒由不去，溫暄不正，春正失時。少陰不遷正，即冷氣不退，春冷後寒，暄暖不時。民病寒熱，四肢煩痛，腰脊強直。木氣雖有餘，位不過於君火也。太陰不遷正，即雲雨失令，萬物枯焦，當生不發。民病手足肢節腫滿，大腹水腫，填臆不食，飧泄脅滿，四肢不舉。雨化欲令，熱猶治之，溫煦於氣，亢而不澤。少陽不遷正，即炎灼弗令，苗莠不榮，酷暑於秋，肅殺晚至，霜露不時。民病㾬瘧骨熱，心悸驚駭，甚時血溢。陽明不遷正，則暑化於前，肅於後，草木反榮。民病寒熱鼽嚏，皮毛折，爪甲枯燋，甚則喘嗽息高，悲傷不樂。熱化乃布，燥化未令，即清勁未行，肺金復病。太陽不遷正，即冬清反寒，易令於春，殺霜在前，寒冰於後，陽光復治，凜冽不作，雰雲待時。民病溫癘至，喉閉溢乾，煩燥而渴，喘息而有音也。寒化待燥，猶治天，氣過失序，與民作災。
		The Emperor said: "I now clearly understand the principle of failure to ascend and descend. I wish to hear about shifting into proper positions—may I know this as well?" Qibo said: "When the proper authority governs the central position, this is called shifting into the correct position. When the Heaven-governing force cannot shift properly, then the previous Heaven-governing authority continues to hold power beyond its term." This means that when the Heaven-governing force is excessive and remains in power for additional days, it continues to govern according to its previous mandate; thus, the new Heaven-governing authority has not yet taken its proper position. If Jueyin does not shift into the proper position, wind and warmth will occur at irregular times; flowers and plants wither prematurely. The people suffer from urinary disorders, eye strain with dizziness, muscle cramps, alternating anger and joy, and red-colored urine. Wind wishes to dominate but cold does not depart; warmth is irregular, and the proper spring conditions are lost at their appointed time. If Shaoyin fails to shift into its proper position, cold Qi does not retreat; spring remains cold and is followed by further chilliness, with warmth occurring at irregular times. The people suffer from chills and fever, pain and discomfort in their limbs, and stiffness in the waist and spine. Although Wood Qi may still be excessive, its position does not surpass that of Sovereign Fire. If Taiyin fails to shift into the proper position, clouds and rain occur at improper times; all things wither and dry up, and those that should grow do not sprout. The people suffer from swelling of the hands, feet, and joints; abdominal distension with edema; chest congestion preventing eating; diarrhea after meals, costal fullness, and inability to move their limbs. Rain wishes to occur according to its mandate, but heat still dominates; warmth lingers in the Qi, becoming excessive without nourishing moisture. If Shaoyang fails to shift into the proper position, intense heat does not follow its mandate; crops and weeds do not flourish. Severe summer heat persists into autumn, while 肃杀 (harsh killing) arrives late, causing frost and dew to occur at irregular times. The people suffer from intermittent fevers with bone heat, palpitations, and startling fear; in severe cases, blood overflows. If Yangming fails to shift into the proper position then summer heat prevails at first and is followed by 肃杀 (harsh cold), causing grasses and trees to flourish inappropriately. The people suffer from alternating chills and fever, sneezing with nasal discharge; their skin and hair crack, nails become dry and scorched. In severe cases, they experience coughing, shortness of breath with elevated breathing, sadness, and lack of joy. Heat transformation spreads, but dryness transformation has not yet occurred; thus, clear and invigorating Qi does not take effect, and the Lungs and Metal again fall ill. If Taiyang fails to shift into the proper position winter becomes abnormally cold, its influence extending into spring; killing frosts occur early, followed by severe ice, while warmth returns unexpectedly. Severe chill does not arise as expected, and cloudy mists await their time. The people suffer from warm plagues; their throats close with dryness and overflow, they feel restless and thirsty, and breathe with a sound. Cold transformation awaits dryness; it still governs Heaven, but the Qi has passed its proper time, disrupting order and bringing disaster to the people.

17
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
帝曰：遷正早晚，以命其旨，願聞退位，可得明哉？歧伯曰：所謂不退者，即天數未終，即天數有餘，名曰復布政，故名曰再治天也，即天令如故而不退位也。厥陰不退位，即大風早舉，時雨不降，溼令不化，民病溫疫，疵廢風生，民病皆肢節痛，頭目痛，伏熱內煩，咽喉乾引飲。少陰不退位，即溫生春冬，蟄蟲早至，草木發生，民病膈熱咽乾，血溢驚駭，小便赤澀，丹瘤疹瘡瘍留毒。太陰不退位，而取寒暑不時，埃昏布作，溫令不去，民病四支少力，食飲不下，泄注淋滿，足脛寒，陰痿閉塞，失溺小便數。少陽不退位，即熱生於春，暑迺後化，冬溫不凍，流水不冰，蟄蟲出見，民病少氣，寒熱更作，便血上熱，小腹堅滿，小便赤沃，甚則血溢。陽明不退位，即春生清冷，草木晚榮，寒熱閒作，民病嘔吐暴注，食飲不下，大便乾燥，四肢不舉，目瞑掉眩。
		The Emperor asked: "I now understand the timing of shifting into proper positions, which determines its principle. I wish to hear about vacating one's position—may I know it clearly?" Qibo said: "What is meant by not vacating a position is that the heavenly term has not yet ended; there remains an excess of heavenly time. This is called repeating governance, and thus it is named 'governing Heaven again.' In this case, the mandate of Heaven continues as before without vacating its post." If Jueyin fails to vacate its post, strong winds arise early; timely rains do not fall and damp conditions fail to transform. The people suffer from warm epidemics, with wind-induced impairments causing various disorders. People experience joint pain in their limbs, headache and eye pain, internal heat 烦 (restlessness), dry throat, and a craving for drinks. If Shaoyin fails to vacate its position, warmth arises in spring and winter; burrowing insects emerge early, and grasses and trees sprout prematurely. The people suffer from heat in the chest, dry throat, blood overflow, startling fear, red-colored and obstructed urination, and lingering toxic sores, rashes, and ulcers. If Taiyin fails to vacate its position and cold and heat occur at irregular times, dust and haze spread; warm conditions persist without ceasing. The people suffer from weakness in their limbs, inability to swallow food or drink, diarrhea with retention of fluids, coldness in the calves, Yin deficiency with blockage, urinary incontinence, and frequent urination. If Shaoyang fails to vacate its position then heat arises in spring, followed later by summer heat; winter remains warm without freezing and flowing water does not freeze. Burrowing insects emerge prematurely. The people suffer from shortness of breath, alternating chills and fever; blood is lost through the lower orifices with upper-body heat; their lower abdomen becomes hard and full, urine turns red and profuse, and in severe cases blood overflows. If Yangming fails to vacate its position spring brings cold and cool conditions, causing grasses and trees to bloom late. Chills and fevers alternate; the people suffer from vomiting and sudden diarrhea, inability to swallow food and drink, dry and hard stools, inability to move their limbs properly, drowsiness with vertigo and visual disturbance.

18
打開字典
	本病論:	
帝曰：天歲早晚，余以知之，願聞地數，可得閒乎？歧伯曰：地下遷正升及退位不前之法，即地土產化，萬物失時之化也。
		The Emperor asked: "I already know about the early or late years of Heaven, but I wish to learn about the numbers of Earth—may I hear them?" Qibo said: "The method by which the Earth shifts into proper positions or fails to advance in vacating its position refers to changes in the Earth's soil, and the transformation of all things losing their proper timing."

19
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
帝曰：余聞天地二甲子，十干十二支。上下經緯天地，數有迭移，失守其位，可得昭乎？歧伯曰：失之迭位者，謂雖得歲正，未得正位之司，即四時不節，即生大疫。注《玄珠密語》云：陽年三十年，除六年天刑，計有太過二十四年，除此六年，皆作太過之用，令不然之旨。今言迭支迭位，皆可作其不及也。
		The Emperor said: "I have heard that Heaven and Earth each have two sets of Jiazi, ten heavenly stems and twelve earthly branches." The upper and lower systems weave through Heaven and Earth; their numbers shift in succession. When they lose their proper positions, can this be clearly explained?" Qibo said: "To lose the successive positions means that although one gains the proper year, one does not gain the correct position of authority. This results in the four seasons lacking regulation and gives rise to great epidemics." The annotation in the Xuanzhu Miyu says: "In a yang year cycle of thirty years, subtracting six years of heavenly punishment, there remain twenty-four years of excessive influence. Apart from these six years, all are used as years of excess, which is not according to the principle." Now, when speaking of successive branches and positions, they can all be considered insufficient.

20
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
假令甲子陽年，土運太窒，如癸亥天數有餘者，年雖交得甲子，厥陰猶尚治天，地已遷正，陽明在泉，去歲少陽以作右閒，即厥陰之地陽明，故不相和奉者也。癸巳相會，土運太過，虛反受木勝，故非太過也，何以言土運太過，況黃鐘不應太窒，木既勝而金還復，金既復而少陰如至，既木勝如火而金復微，如此則甲己失守，後三年化成土疫，晚至於卯，早至丙寅，土疫至也，大小善惡，推其天地，詳乎太一。又只如甲子年，如甲至子而合，應交司而治天，即下己未遷正，而戊寅少陽未退位者，亦甲己下有合也，即土運非太過，而木乃乘虛而勝土也，金次又行復勝之，即反邪化也。陰陽天地殊異爾，故其大小善惡，一如天地之法旨也。
		Suppose, for example, that the year Jiazi is a yang year and the Earth element's influence is excessively obstructed. If the previous year Guihai still has an excess of heavenly numbers, then although this year marks the transition to Jiazi, Jueyin still governs Heaven while the Earth has already shifted into its proper position. Yangming now resides below, and last year's Shaoyang remains in the right position. Thus, the earthly position of Jueyin is occupied by Yangming; therefore, they do not harmonize or support one another. If Gui Si coincide, the Earth element is excessively strong. However, emptiness instead suffers from Wood dominance; thus it cannot be considered excessive. Why then speak of an excessive Earth influence? Moreover, Huangzhong should not be overly obstructed. When Wood dominates, Metal returns to counterbalance it; when Metal returns, Shaoyin arrives as expected. If Wood's dominance resembles Fire and Metal becomes weak, this indicates that Jiaji have failed in their positions. Three years later, the transformation manifests as an Earth epidemic, arriving late at Mao or early at Bingyin. The arrival of the Earth epidemic is marked; whether it brings great or minor good or evil depends on the interplay between Heaven and Earth, which must be carefully examined through Taiyi. Again, for example, in the year Jiazi: if Jia reaches Zi and they combine, it should transition to assume authority over Heaven. If at this time Jiwei has already shifted into its correct position but Wu Yin's Shaoyang has not yet vacated its post, then there is still a combination between Jia and Ji below. In such a case, the Earth influence is not excessive; instead, Wood takes advantage of the emptiness to dominate over Earth. Metal follows in turn to counteract this dominance, which results in an abnormal transformation. The Yin and Yang of Heaven and Earth are distinct; therefore, whether great or minor, good or evil, they follow the principles of Heaven and Earth exactly.

打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
假令丙寅陽年太過，如乙丑天數有餘者，雖交得丙寅，太陰尚治天也，地已遷正，厥陰司地，去歲太陽以作右閒，即天太陰而地厥，故地不奉天化也。乙辛相會，水運太虛，反受土勝，故非太過，即太簇之管，太羽不應，土勝而雨化，水復即風，此者丙辛失守其會，後三年化成水疫，晚至己巳，早至戊辰，甚即速，微即徐，水疫至也，大小善惡推其天地數，乃太乙游宮。又只丙寅年，丙至寅且合，應交司而治天，即辛巳未得遷正，而庚辰太陽未退位者，亦丙辛不合德也，即水運亦小虛而小勝，或有復，後三年化癘，名曰水癘，其狀如水疫，治法如前。
		Suppose, for instance, that Bingyin is a yang year of excessive influence. If Yichou still has an excess in the heavenly numbers, then even though this year transitions to Bingyin, Taiyin continues to govern Heaven while the Earth position has already shifted into proper order and Jueyin now governs the Earth. Last year's Taiyang remains in the right position; thus, Heaven is governed by Taiyin while the Earth belongs to Jueyin. Therefore, the Earth does not support or follow the transformation of Heaven. If Yi and Xin coincide, the Water element is excessively weak. Instead of being strong, it suffers from Earth dominance; therefore, this cannot be considered excessive influence. In this case, Taicuo should govern, but Taiyu does not respond as expected. When Earth dominates, rain transforms accordingly; when Water returns to balance, wind arises. This indicates that Bingxin have failed in their coordination. Three years later the transformation manifests as a Water epidemic, arriving late at Jisi or early at Wuchen. The speed of its arrival depends on severity—rapid if severe, slow if mild. When the Water epidemic arrives, whether it brings great or small good or evil must be determined by examining the numbers of Heaven and Earth through Taiyi's movement among palaces. Again, in the case of the year Bingyin: if Bing reaches Yin and they combine as expected to assume governance over Heaven, yet Xin Si has not yet shifted into its proper position and Gengchen's Taiyang has not vacated its post—this indicates that Bingxin also fail to harmonize their virtues. In this situation, the Water influence is slightly weak but still suffers from dominance; there may be a subsequent return of balance. Three years later an epidemic will arise, named "Shuiyi," with symptoms resembling those of a Water epidemic. Its treatment follows the same methods as before.

打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
假令庚辰陽年太過，如己卯天數有餘者，雖交庚辰年也，陽明猶尚治天，地以遷正，太陰司地，去歲少陰以作右閒，即天陽明而地太陰也，故地下奉天也。乙巳相會，金運太虛，反受火勝，故非太過也，即姑洗之管，太商不應，火勝熱化，水復寒刑，此乙庚失守，其後三年化成金疫也，速至壬午，徐至癸未，金疫至也，大小善惡，推本年天數及太一也。又只如庚辰，如庚至辰，且應交司而治天，即下乙未未得遷正者，即地甲午少陰未退位者，且乙庚不合德也，即下乙未，干失剛，亦金運小虛也，有小勝或無復，後三年化癘，名曰金癘，其狀如金疫也，治法如前。
		Suppose, for example, the year Gengchen is a yang year of excessive dominance. If Jimao still has an excess in heavenly numbers, then although it transitions into the year Gengchen, Yangming continues to govern Heaven; meanwhile, the Earth position has already shifted properly and Taiyin now governs Earth. Last year's Shaoyin remains in the right position—thus, Heaven is governed by Yangming while Earth belongs to Taiyin. Therefore, below does not follow or support above. If Yi and Si coincide, the Metal element is excessively weak; instead of being strong it suffers from Fire dominance. Therefore this cannot be considered excessive influence—this corresponds to Guxian's regulation, where Taishang should respond but does not. When Fire dominates heat transforms accordingly, and when Water returns cold punishment arises. This indicates a failure in coordination between Yi and Geng. Three years later an epidemic of Metal will arise; it may arrive rapidly at Renwu or slowly at Guiwei. The arrival of the Metal epidemic is marked—whether great or minor good or evil must be traced back to this year's heavenly numbers and Taiyi. Again, for example in the year Gengchen: if Geng reaches Chen and should transition to assume governance over Heaven but Jiwei has not yet shifted properly into its position—meaning that Jiawu's Shaoyin has also failed to vacate its post—and Yi and Geng fail to harmonize their virtue, then Jiwei's stem is weak. In this case the Metal influence is slightly weakened; there may be slight dominance or no return of balance at all. Three years later an epidemic arises, named "Jinyi," with symptoms resembling a Metal epidemic. Its treatment follows methods as previously described.

23
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
假令壬午陽年太過，如辛巳天數有餘者，雖交後壬午年也，厥陰猶尚治天，地巳遷正，陽明在泉，去歲丙申少陽以作右閒，即天厥陰而地陽明，故地不奉天者也。丁辛相合會，木運太虛，反受金勝，故非太過也，即蕤賓之管，太角不應，金行燥勝，火化熱復甚即速，微即徐，疫至大小善惡，推疫至之年天數及太一。又只如壬至午，且應交司而治之，即下丁酉未得遷正者，即地下丙申少陽未得退位者，見丁壬不合德也，即丁柔干失剛，亦木運小虛也，有小勝小復。後三年化癘，名曰木癘，其狀如風疫，法治如前。
		Suppose, for example, Renwu is a yang year of excessive strength. If Xin Si still has an excess in its heavenly numbers, then although the transition to the year Renwu occurs, Jueyin continues to govern Heaven, while the Earth position has already properly shifted and Yangming now resides below. Last year's Bing Shen Shaoyang remains on the right side—thus, Heaven belongs to Jueyin and Earth belongs to Yangming; therefore, the Earth does not follow or support Heaven. When Ding and Xin combine, the Wood element is excessively weak and instead suffers from Metal dominance; therefore this cannot be considered excessive. This corresponds to Ruibin's regulation where Taijiao should respond but does not—Metal becomes dry and dominant, while Fire transforms into heat. The epidemic arrives rapidly if severe or slowly if mild. Whether it brings great or minor good fortune depends on the heavenly numbers of the year in which the epidemic occurs as well as Taiyi. Again, for example when Ren reaches Wu and should transition to assume its governance—yet Diyou has not yet properly shifted into position, nor has Bing Shen's Shaoyang vacated its post. This shows that Ding and Ren fail to harmonize in virtue; the stem of Di is weak and lacks strength, indicating a slight deficiency in Wood influence with minor dominance or return. Three years later an epidemic arises named "Muyi," whose symptoms resemble those of a wind-related plague, and its treatment follows the methods previously described.

24
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
假令戊申陽年太過，如丁未天數太過者，雖交得入申年也，太陰尚治天，地已遷正，厥陰在泉，去歲壬戌太陽以退位作右閒，即天丁未，地癸亥，故地不奉天化也。丁癸相會，火運太虛反受火勝，故非太過也，即夷則之管，上太徵不應，此戊癸失守其會，後三年化疫也，速至庚戌，大小善惡，推疫至之年天數及大一。又只如戊申，如戊至申，且應交司而治天，即下癸亥未得遷正者，即地下壬戌太陽未退位者，見戊癸未合德也，即下癸柔干失剛，見火運小虛也，有小勝或無復也，後三年化癘名曰火癘也，治法如前，治之法可寒之泄之。
		Suppose, for example, Wushen is a yang year of excessive power. If Dingwei has an excess in its heavenly number, then although the year transitions into Shen, Taiyin still governs Heaven; meanwhile, Earth has already shifted to proper order and Jueyinfu now resides below. Last year's Ren Xu Taiyang vacated its position and remains on the right—thus, Heaven corresponds to Dingwei while Earth belongs to Guihai. Therefore, Earth does not follow or respond to Heaven's transformation. When Ding and Gui coincide, the Fire element is excessively weak but instead suffers from Fire dominance; therefore it cannot be considered excessive—this corresponds to Yize's regulation where Taizheng should respond but does not. This indicates a failure of coordination between Wu and Gui. Three years later an epidemic occurs, arriving rapidly at Geng Xu. Whether great or minor good fortune results depends on the heavenly number of the year when the epidemic arrives as well as Taiyi (the Great One). Again, for example with Wushen: if Wu reaches Shen and should transition to govern Heaven but Guihai has not yet shifted into proper position—nor has Ren Xu's Taiyang vacated its post—it indicates that Wu and Gui have failed in their virtue. The stem of Gui is weak and lacks strength; this shows a slight deficiency in the Fire influence, with minor dominance or no return at all. Three years later it transforms into an epidemic named "Huoyi," which should be treated as previously described—its treatment involves cooling and purging methods.

25
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	本病論:	
黃帝曰：人氣不足，天氣如虛，人神失守，神光不聚，邪鬼干人，致有夭亡，可得聞乎？歧伯曰：人之五藏，一藏不足，又會天虛，感邪之至也。人憂愁思慮即傷心，又或遇少陰司天，天數不及，太陰作接閒至，即謂天虛也，此即人氣天氣同虛也。又遇驚而奪精，汗出於心，因而三虛，神明失守，心為君主之官，神明出焉，神失守位，即神游上丹田，在帝太一帝君泥九君下，神既失守，神光不聚，卻遇火不及之歲，有黑尸鬼見之，令人暴亡。人飲食勞倦即傷脾，又或遇太陰司天，天數不及，即少陽作接閒至，即謂之虛也，此即人氣虛而天氣虛也。又遇飲食飽甚，汗出於胃，醉飽行房，汗出於脾，因而三虛，脾神失守，脾為諫議之官，智周出焉，神既失守，神光失位而不聚也，卻遇土不及之年，或己年或甲年失守，或太陰天虛，青尸鬼見之，令人卒亡。人久坐溼地，強力入水即傷腎，腎為作強之官，伎巧出焉，因而三虛，腎神失守，神志失位，神光不聚，卻遇水不及之年，或辛不會符，或丙年失守，或太陽司天虛，有黃尸鬼至，見之令人暴亡。人或恚怒，氣逆上而不下，即傷肝也。又遇厥陰司天，天數不及，即少陰作接閒至，是謂天虛也，此謂天虛人虛也。又遇疾走恐懼，汗出於肝，肝為將軍之官，謀慮出焉，神位失守，神光不聚，又遇木不及年，或丁年不符，或壬年失守，或厥陰司天虛也，有白尸鬼見之，令人暴亡也。已上五失守者，天虛而人虛也，神游失守其位，即有五尸鬼干人，令人暴亡，也謂之曰尸厥。人犯五神易位，即神光不圓也，非但尸鬼，即一切邪犯者，皆是神失守位故也。此謂得守者生，失守者死，得神昌，失神者亡。
		The Yellow Emperor asked: "When human Qi is insufficient and the Heaven's Qi appears empty, when human spirits fail to hold their posts and spiritual radiance does not gather, evil ghosts interfere with people, causing premature death. May I hear about this?" Qibo said: "When one of the five Zang organs in a person is insufficient, and it coincides with an emptiness in Heaven's Qi, this leads to susceptibility to pathogenic influences." When a person experiences worry, sorrow, and overthinking it harms the Heart. If they also encounter Shaoyin governing Heaven with insufficient heavenly numbers, and Taiyin intervenes as an intermediary—this is called "heavenly emptiness." In this case both human Qi and Heaven's Qi are simultaneously deficient. If one also experiences sudden fright that depletes essence, causing perspiration to arise from the Heart, this results in three deficiencies. When spiritual clarity fails to hold its position—the Heart, as the official of sovereign authority, is where spirit and wisdom originate—when the spirit loses its post it roams upward into the Shangdantian (upper elixir field), beneath the Emperor Taiyi, the Lord Di Jun, and Ni Jiu Jun. When the spirit fails to hold position, spiritual radiance does not coalesce; if this coincides with a year of insufficient Fire influence, black corpse ghosts appear, causing sudden death in people. When a person suffers from overeating, fatigue, or exhaustion it harms the Spleen. If they also encounter Taiyin governing Heaven with insufficient heavenly number and Shaoyang intervenes as an intermediary, this is called "emptiness." In such cases both human Qi and Heaven's Qi are deficient. If one also consumes excessive food and drinks, causing perspiration from the Stomach; or becomes drunk and engages in sexual activity, leading to perspiration from the Spleen—this results in three deficiencies. The spirit of the Spleen then fails to hold its post. As the Spleen is the official of deliberation and advice, it gives rise to wisdom and discernment. When this spirit loses its position, spiritual radiance departs its place and does not coalesce. If this coincides with a year when Earth influence is insufficient—such as in the years Ji or Jia where there has been failure in governance—or if Taiyin's heavenly emptiness occurs, then Green Corpse Ghosts appear, causing sudden death. When a person sits for long periods on damp ground or forcefully enters water, it harms the Kidneys. The Kidney is the official of strength and vigor, from which skill and dexterity arise. This leads to three deficiencies; the spirit of the Kidney then fails in its post, losing its position. When spiritual willpower departs from its place, spiritual radiance no longer coalesces. If this coincides with years when Water influence is insufficient—such as when Xin fails to align properly or Bing year governance has failed—or if Taiyang governing Heaven is empty, Yellow Corpse Ghosts appear and cause sudden death in people who encounter them. When a person experiences anger or resentment, Qi rebels upward without descending—it harms the Liver. If they also encounter Jueyin governing Heaven, with insufficient heavenly numbers and Shaoyin intervening as an intermediary—this is termed "heavenly emptiness." In this case both Heaven and the person are in a state of deficiency. If one also engages in rapid running or experiences fear, causing perspiration from the Liver—the Liver is the official of generals, where strategy and deliberation arise—when its spiritual position fails to hold and spiritual radiance does not coalescence, if this coincides with years of insufficient Wood influence, such as when Ding year alignment is incorrect or Ren year governance has failed, or Jueyin governing Heaven is empty—White Corpse Ghosts appear causing sudden death in those who encounter them. The above five cases of lost governance represent both Heaven's emptiness and human deficiency. When the spirit roams without holding its position, it results in five Corpse Ghosts interfering with people, causing sudden death—this is known as "Shi jue." When a person violates the five spirits' proper positions, spiritual radiance becomes incomplete. Not only Corpse Ghosts but all kinds of pathogenic influences arise due to the spirit's failure in holding its position. This means that those who maintain their spiritual positions live, while those who lose them die; those who preserve their spirits flourish, and those who lose their spirits perish. 

74. 至真要大論 Zhenzhenyao Dalian (The Great Discourse on the Essentials of True Principles)

1
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	至真要大... :	
黃帝問曰：五氣交合，盈虛更作，余知之矣。六氣分治，司天地者，其至何如。歧伯再拜對曰：明乎哉問也。天地之大紀，人神之通應也。帝曰：願聞上合昭昭，下合冥冥，柰何。歧伯曰：此道之所主，工之所疑也。帝曰：願聞其道也。歧伯曰：厥陰司天，其化以風，少陰司天，其化以熱，太陰司天，其化以濕，少陽司天，其化以火，陽明司天，其化以燥，太陽司天，其化以寒，以所臨藏位，命其病者也。帝曰：地化柰何。歧伯曰：司天同候，間氣皆然。帝曰：間氣何謂。歧伯曰：司左右者，是謂間氣也。帝曰：何以異之。歧伯曰：主歲者紀歲，間氣者紀步也。帝曰：善。歲主奈何。歧伯曰：厥陰司天為風化，在泉為酸化，司氣為蒼化，閒氣為動化。少陰司天為熱化，在泉為苦化，不司氣化，居氣為灼化。太陰司天為濕化，在泉為甘化，司氣為黅化，閒氣為柔化。少陽司天為火化，在泉為苦化，司氣為丹化，間氣為明化。陽明司天為燥化，在泉為辛化，司氣為素化，間氣為清化。太陽司天為寒化，在泉為鹹化，司氣為玄化，間氣為藏化。故治病者，必明六化分治，五味五色所生，五藏所宜，迺可以言盈虛病生之緒也。
		The Yellow Emperor asked: "The five qi intermingle and combine, waxing and waning alternately; I know this already." The six qi govern separately, those in charge of heaven and earth—how do they arrive? Qibo bowed twice in reply: "How clear your question is." It is the great principle of heaven and earth, the mutual correspondence between humans and spirits. The Emperor said: "I wish to hear how the upper harmonizes with what is bright and clear, and the lower harmonizes with what is obscure and hidden. How does this happen?" Qibo said: "This is a matter governed by the Dao, something that medical practitioners find puzzling." The Emperor said: "I wish to hear about this principle." Qibo said: "When Jueyin governs the heavens, its transformation is by wind; when Shaoyin governs the heavens, its change is by heat; when Taiyin governs the heavens, it transforms through dampness; when Shaoyang governs the heavens, transformation occurs via fire; when Yangming governs the heavens, dryness brings about change; and when Taiyang governs the heavens, cold causes transformation. The diseases are named according to which zang organ is affected by these influences." The Emperor asked: "What of the transformations on earth?" Qibo said: "The signs governing heaven are observed similarly, and so it is with the intercalary qi." The Emperor asked: "What do you mean by 'intercalary qi'?" Qibo said: "Those that govern the left and right are called intercalary qi." The Emperor asked: "What makes them different?" Qibo said: "The one governing the year records the year, while the intercalary qi records the steps." The Emperor said: "Good." What about the ruler of the year? Qibo said: "When Jueyin governs heaven, it brings about wind transformation; when in the lower region, it causes sour transformation; as governing qi, it produces green transformation; and as intercalary qi, it results in movement transformation." Shaoyin governing heaven brings about heat transformation; when in the lower regions, it causes bitter transformation. It does not govern qi transformation; as resident qi, it produces scorching transformation. Taiyin governing heaven brings about damp transformation; when in the lower regions it causes sweet transformation; as governing qi it produces yellow transformation; and as intercalary Qi, it results in softening transformation. Shaoyang governing heaven brings about fire transformation; when in the lower regions causes bitter transformation; as governing qi produces red transformation; and as intercalary qi, results in bright transformation. Yangming governing heaven brings about dryness transformation; when in the lower regions produces pungent transformation; as governing qi causes white transformation; and as intercalary QI, results in clear transformation. Taiyang governing heaven brings about cold transformation; when in the lower regions leads to salty transformation; as governing qi results in dark transformation; and as intercalaryqi, causes zang organ transformation. Therefore, those who treat diseases must understand the six transformations governing separately, the five flavors and five colors they produce, and what is appropriate for the five zang organs; only then can one discuss the patterns of excesses, deficiencies, and disease onset.

2
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	至真要大... :	
帝曰：厥陰在泉而酸化，先余知之矣。風化之行也何如。歧伯曰：風行于地，所謂本也，餘氣同法。本乎天者，天之氣也，本乎地者，地之氣也，天地合氣，六節分而萬物化生矣。故曰：謹候氣宜，無失病機，此之謂也。帝曰：其主病何如。歧伯曰：司歲備物，則無遺主矣。帝曰：先歲物何也。歧伯曰：天地之專精也。帝曰：司氣者何如。歧伯曰：司氣者主歲同，然有餘不足也。帝曰：非司歲物何謂也。歧伯曰：散也，故質同而異等也，氣味有薄厚，性用有躁靜，治保有多少，力化有淺深，此之謂也。帝曰：歲主藏害何謂。歧伯曰：以所不勝命之則其要也。帝曰：治之柰何。歧伯曰：上淫于下，所勝平之，外淫于內，所勝治之。帝曰：善。平氣何如。歧伯曰：謹察陰陽所在而調之，以平為期，正者正治，反者反治。
		The Emperor said: "When Jueyin is in the lower region and sour transformation occurs, I already know this." What is it like for wind transformation to manifest? Qibo said: "Wind moves on earth; this is called the root. The remaining qi follows the same principle." That which originates from heaven is heavenly qi; that which originates from earth is earthly qi. When the qi of heaven and earth combine, the six divisions emerge, and all things are transformed and born. Therefore it is said: "Carefully observe the appropriate qi; do not miss the signs of disease." This is what that means. The Emperor asked: "What illnesses does each govern?" Qibo said: "If one prepares for the year by understanding all things, there will be no oversight in determining what governs." The Emperor asked: "What is meant by 'preparing for the year's substances'?" Qibo said: "It refers to the concentrated essence of heaven and earth." The Emperor asked: "What about those who govern qi?" Qibo said: "Those governing qi are the same as those governing the year, yet they also have excesses and deficiencies." The Emperor asked: "What is meant by things not governed by the year?" Qibo said: "They are scattered, hence their substances may be similar but their qualities differ. Their flavors and qi have varying degrees of strength; their natures and functions range from active to tranquil; the extent of treatment and preservation varies in amount; and their power to transform differs in depth—this is what it means." The Emperor asked: "What does 'the ruler of the year harms the zang organs' mean?" Qibo said: "Naming it according to what it cannot overcome is its essential principle." The Emperor asked: "How should one treat such conditions?" Qibo said: "When the upper excess invades downward, balance it with what it cannot overcome; when an external excess affects internally, treat it with what it cannot overcome." The Emperor said: "Good." What about balanced qi? Qibo said: "Carefully observe where yin and yang reside, and regulate them with the goal of balance. For conditions that are straightforward, apply direct treatment; for those that are inverted, use counter-treatment."

3
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	至真要大... :	
帝曰：夫子言察陰陽所在而調之，論言人迎與寸口相應，若引繩小大齊等，命曰平，陰之所在寸口何如。歧伯曰：視歲南北，可知之矣。帝曰：願卒聞之。歧伯曰：北政之歲，少陰在泉，則寸口不應，厥陰在泉，則右不應，太陰在泉，則左不應。南政之歲，少陰司天，則寸口不應，厥陰司天，則右不應，太陰司天，則左不應。諸不應者，反其診則見矣。帝曰：尺候何如。歧伯曰：北政之歲，三陰在下，則寸不應，三陰在上，則尺不應。南政之歲，三陰在天，則寸不應，三陰在泉，則尺不應，左右同。故曰：知其要者，一言而終，不知其要，流散無窮，此之謂也。
		The Emperor asked: "You said to observe where yin and yang are located and regulate accordingly. The text states that the Renying pulse and Cunkou pulse correspond, like a drawn rope with small and large parts equally matched, which is called balance. Where does this apply in terms of yin's location at the Cunkou?" Qibo said: "By observing whether the year belongs to the north or south, you can know it." The Emperor said: "I wish to hear this in full." Qibo said: "In the years of northern governance, when Shaoyin is in the lower regions, then the Cunkou does not respond; when Jueyin is in the lowers regions, the right side does not respond; and when Taiyin is in the lowe regions, the left side does not respond." In the years of southern governance, when Shaoyin governs heaven, then Cunkou does not respond. When Jueyin governs Heaven, the right side does not correspond; and when Taiyin governs Heaven, The left side does not correspond. Those that do not respond can be understood by reversing the diagnosis—then they become evident. The Emperor asked: "What about the measurement of the pulse at the chi?" Qibo said: "In years of northern governance, if the three yin are below, then Cunkou will not respond; if the three yin are above, then Chi will not respond." In years of southern governance, if the three Yin are in heaven, then Cunkou will not correspond; if the three Yin are below, then Chi will not correspond. This applies equally to both left and right sides. Therefore it is said: "He who understands the essentials can summarize in one sentence; he who does not understand them will find his thoughts scattered endlessly." This is what that means.

4
打開字典
顯示相似段落
	至真要大... :	
帝曰：善。天地之氣，內淫而病何如。歧伯曰：歲厥陰在泉，風淫所勝，則地氣不明，平野昧，草迺早秀，民病洒洒振寒，善伸數欠，心痛支滿，兩脇裏急，飲食不下，鬲咽不通，食則嘔，腹脹善噫，得後與氣，則快然如衰，身體皆重。歲少陰在泉，熱淫所勝，則焰浮川澤，陰處反明，民病腹中常鳴，氣上衝胸，喘不能久立，寒熱皮膚痛，目瞑齒痛䪼腫，惡寒發熱如瘧，少腹中痛腹大，蟄蟲不藏。歲太陰在泉，草迺早榮，濕淫所勝，則埃昏巖谷黃反見黑，至陰之交民病飲積心痛耳聾，渾渾焞焞，嗌腫喉痺，陰病血見，少腹痛腫，不得小便，病衝頭痛，目似脫，項似拔，腰似折，髀不可以回，膕如結，腨如別。歲少陽在泉，火淫所勝，則焰明郊野，寒熱更至，民病注泄赤白，少腹痛溺赤，甚則血便，少陰同候。歲陽明在泉，燥淫所勝，則霿霧清暝，民病喜嘔，嘔有苦善大息，心脅痛不能反側，甚則嗌乾面塵，身無膏澤，足外反熱。歲太陽在泉，寒淫所勝則凝肅慘慄，民病少腹控睾，引腰脊上衝心痛，血見，嗌痛頷腫。帝曰：善。治之柰何。歧伯曰：諸氣在泉，風淫于內，治以辛涼，佐以苦，以甘緩之，以辛散之。熱淫于內，治以鹹寒，佐以甘苦，以酸收之，以苦發之。濕淫于內，治以苦熱，佐以酸淡，以苦燥之，以淡泄之。火淫于內，治以鹹冷，佐以苦辛，以酸收之，以苦發之。燥淫于內，治以苦溫，佐以甘辛，以苦下之。寒淫于內，治以甘熱，佐以苦辛，以鹹寫之，以辛潤之，以苦堅之。
		The Emperor said: "Good." What is it like when the qi of heaven and earth invades internally and causes disease? Qibo said: "In the year when Jueyin is below, if wind excess prevails and overcomes its counterpart, then earthly qi becomes obscure; plains become dim; grasses mature early; people suffer from chills with shivering, frequent stretching and sighing, chest pain and fullness in the limbs, tightness between the two flanks inside, inability to swallow food, obstruction of the epigastrium and throat, vomiting after eating, abdominal distension with frequent belching. After defecation or passing gas, they feel momentarily relieved as if their condition has lessened; yet their entire body feels heavy." In the year when Shaoyin is below, if heat excess prevails and overpowers its counterpart, then flames rise over rivers and marshes; dark places become unexpectedly bright. People suffer from constant gurgling in their abdomen, qi surging upward to the chest, shortness of breath preventing prolonged standing, chills and fever with skin pain, drowsiness and toothache with swelling of the cheeks, aversion to cold and intermittent fevers resembling malaria, pain in the lower abdomen and abdominal distension, and burrowing insects failing to remain hidden. In the year when Taiyin is below, grasses flourish early. If damp excess prevails and overcomes its counterbalance, then dust obscures mountains and valleys; yellow appears as black. At the meeting of yin extremes, people suffer from fluid retention with accumulation, heart pain, deafness, a confused and dim state of mind, swelling in the throat and obstruction of the pharynx, internal disorders manifesting through blood loss, lower abdominal pain and swelling, inability to urinate, headaches as if something were piercing the head, eyes feeling like they are about to pop out, the neck feeling as though it is being pulled upward, the waist feeling broken, the hip joints unable to turn, knees stiff as if bound together, and calves feeling separated. In the year when Shaoyang is below, if fire excess prevails and overcomes its opponent, then flames illuminate the countryside; alternating chills and fevers occur. People suffer from dysentery with red or white discharge, lower abdominal pain, reddish urine, and in severe cases, blood in the stool. The signs are similar to those of Shaoyin. In the year when Yangming is below, if dryness excess prevails and overcomes its opposite, then mists and fog obscure clarity; people suffer from frequent vomiting with a bitter taste in their mouths, deep sighing, pain in the heart and flanks preventing them from turning or lying on either side. In severe cases, they experience dry throat, dusty complexion, lack of body moisture, and heat in the soles of the feet. In the year when Taiyang is below, if cold excess prevails and overcomes its opposing force, then things become congealed and severe, with a bleak and shivering atmosphere. People suffer from pain in the lower abdomen extending to the testicles, pulling up the waist and spine toward the heart causing chest pain, blood loss, sore throat, and swelling of the jaw. The Emperor said: "Good." How should these conditions be treated? Qibo said: "When all the qi are below and wind excess invades internally, treat with pungent-cool substances; assist with bitter ones. Use sweet to relieve tension, and pungent to disperse." When heat excess invades internally, treat it with salty-cold substances; assist with sweet and bitter ones. Use sour to astringe, and bitter to promote eruption. When damp excess invades internally, treat with bitter-warm substances; assist with sour and bland ones. Use bitter to dry it out, and bland to eliminate it. When fire excess invades internally, treat with salty-cold substances; support with bitter and pungent ones. Use sour to constrict it, and bitter to induce eruption. When dryness excess invades internally, treat with warm-bitter substances; assist with sweet and pungent ones, using bitter to purge it. When cold excess invades internally, treat with sweet-warm substances; assist with bitter and pungent. Use salty to purge it, pungent to moisten it, and bitter to strengthen it.

打開字典
顯示相似段落
	至真要大... :	
帝曰：善。天氣之變何如。歧伯曰：厥陰司天，風淫所勝，則太虛埃昏，雲物以擾，寒生春氣，流水不冰，民病胃脘當心而痛，上支兩脅，鬲咽不通，飲食不下，舌本強，食則嘔，冷泄腹脹溏泄瘕水閉，蟄蟲不去，病本于脾。衝陽絕，死不治。少陰司天，熱淫所勝，怫熱至火行其政，民病胸中煩熱嗌乾，右胠滿，皮膚痛，寒熱欬喘，大雨且至，唾血血泄，鼽衂嚏嘔，溺色變，甚則瘡瘍胕腫，肩背臂臑及缺盆中痛，心痛肺䐜，腹大滿膨，膨而喘欬，病本于肺，尺澤絕，死不治。太陰司天，濕淫所勝，則沈陰且布，雨變枯槁，胕腫骨痛陰痺，陰痺者按之不得，腰脊頭項痛時眩，大便難，陰氣不用，飢不欲食，欬唾則有血，心如懸，病本于腎。太谿絕，死不治。少陽司天，火淫所勝，則溫氣流行，金政不平，民病頭痛發熱惡寒而瘧，熱上皮膚痛，色變黃赤，傳而為水，身面胕腫，腹滿仰息，泄注赤白，瘡瘍欬唾血，煩心胸中熱，甚則鼽衂，病本于肺，天府絕，死不治。陽明司天，燥淫所勝，則木迺晚榮，草迺晚生，筋骨內變，民病左胠脇痛，寒清于中感而瘧，大涼革候，欬，腹中鳴注泄鶩溏，名木歛生菀于下，草焦上首，心脅暴痛，不可反側，嗌乾面塵腰痛，丈夫㿉疝，婦人少腹痛，目昧眥，瘍瘡痤癰，蟄蟲來見，病本于肝。太衝絕，死不治。太陽司天，寒淫所勝，則寒氣反至，水且冰，血變于中，發為癰瘍，民病厥心痛，嘔血血泄鼽衂，善悲時眩仆，運火炎烈，雨暴迺雹，胸腹滿，手熱肘攣，掖衝，心澹澹大動，胸脇胃脘不安，面赤目黃，善噫嗌乾，甚則色炱，渴而欲飲，病本于心。神門絕，死不治。所謂動氣知其藏也。帝曰：善。治之柰何。歧伯曰：司天之氣，風淫所勝，平以辛涼，佐以苦甘，以甘緩之，以酸寫之。熱淫所勝，平以鹹寒，佐以苦甘，以酸收之。濕淫所勝，平以苦熱，佐以酸辛，以苦燥之，以淡泄之。濕上甚而熱，治以苦溫，佐以甘辛，以汗為故而止。火淫所勝，平以酸冷，佐以苦甘，以酸收之，以苦發之，以酸復之，熱淫同。燥淫所勝，平以苦濕，佐以酸辛，以苦下之。寒淫所勝，平以辛熱，佐以甘苦，以鹹寫之。
		The Emperor said: "Good." What about changes in the qi of heaven? Qibo said: "When Jueyin governs in the heavens and wind excess prevails, then the vast sky becomes dusty and dim, clouds and objects become disturbed. Cold gives rise to spring qi; flowing water does not freeze. People suffer from pain at the stomach region right under the heart, upward extension of both flanks, obstruction of the diaphragm and throat preventing swallowing, inability to take in food or drink, stiffness at the base of the tongue, vomiting after eating, cold diarrhea with abdominal distension and loose stools, accumulation of water and blockage, burrowing insects not retreating—this illness originates from the spleen." If the Chongyang pulse is cut off, death occurs and it cannot be treated. When Shaoyin governs in the heavens, and heat excess prevails, oppressive heat arises as fire exerts its authority. People suffer from 烦热 (restless heat) in the chest, dry throat, fullness on the right side of the hypochondrium, skin pain, chills with fever, coughing, and shortness of breath; heavy rain is about to come. They may also experience hemoptysis, bloody diarrhea, nasal discharge, nosebleeds, sneezing, vomiting, and changes in urine color. In severe cases, sores and swellings appear, with pain in the shoulders, back, arms, forearms, and the hollow of the clavicle; chest pain, lung distension, abdominal swelling and fullness, bloating with coughing and shortness of breath—this illness originates from the lungs. If the Chize pulse is cut off, it leads to death and cannot be treated. When Taiyin governs in heaven and damp excess prevails, the atmosphere becomes heavy with gloom spreading widely; rain causes dryness and withering. People suffer from swelling, bone pain, and yin obstruction—this "yin obstruction" means that pressing on it brings no relief. They experience pain in the waist, spine, head, and neck, occasional dizziness, difficulty passing stools, inability to use yin qi properly, hunger without desire for food, blood when coughing or spitting, a sensation as if the heart is suspended—this illness originates from the kidneys. If the Taixi pulse is cut off, death will occur and it cannot be treated. When Shaoyang governs in the heavens and fire excess prevails, warm qi spreads widely while metal's authority becomes unbalanced. People suffer from headaches, fever with chills resembling malaria; heat rises to cause skin pain, yellowish-red discoloration of the complexion, which may progress into water retention, resulting in swelling of the body and face, abdominal distension forcing them to breathe upward, dysentery with red and white discharge, sores, coughing up blood, restlessness, and internal heat. In severe cases, there are nosebleeds—this illness originates from the lungs; if the Tianfu pulse is cut off, death results and it cannot be treated. When Yangming governs in the heavens and dryness excess prevails, trees bloom late, grasses grow late; internal changes occur in tendons and bones. People suffer from pain on the left hypochondrium and flanks, cold sensations within the body leading to malaria-like symptoms. A sudden cool climate replaces previous conditions, causing coughing, gurgling sounds in the abdomen with diarrhea and loose stools. Trees contract their growth at lower levels while grasses dry out at upper parts; people experience sudden chest and flank pain so severe they cannot turn or lie on either side, a dry throat, dusty face, and waist pain. Men suffer from hernias, women from lower abdominal pain; eyes feel dim with redness of the eyelids; boils and abscesses appear, burrowing insects emerge—this illness originates in the liver. If the Taichong pulse is cut off, death ensues and it cannot be treated. When Taiyang governs in the heavens and cold excess prevails, cold qi returns unexpectedly; water freezes. Blood undergoes internal changes, leading to abscesses and sores. People suffer from sudden heart pain with vomiting of blood or bloody diarrhea, nosebleeds, frequent sadness, occasional dizziness and fainting. Fire movement becomes intense, causing heavy rain followed by hail. Symptoms include fullness in the chest and abdomen, hot hands, elbow cramps, armpit congestion, a feeling of great agitation in the heart, discomfort in the chest, flanks, and stomach region, red face with yellow eyes, frequent belching, dry throat; in severe cases, complexion darkens like soot, intense thirst with desire to drink—this illness originates from the heart. If the Shenmen pulse is cut off, death ensues and it cannot be treated. This is what is meant by understanding which zang organ a movement of qi affects. The Emperor said: "Good." How should these conditions be treated? Qibo said: "For the qi governing heaven, when wind excess prevails and dominates, treat with pungent and cool substances; assist with bitter-sweet ones. Use sweet to alleviate tension, and sour to purge." When heat excess prevails and dominates, balance with salty-cold substances; assist them with bitter-sweet. Use sour to astringe. When damp excess prevails and dominates, regulate it with bitter-warm substances; support them with sour-pungent. Use bitter to dry, and bland to drain. When dampness is severe on top and heat follows, treat with warm-bitter; assist with sweet-pungent. Stop the condition by inducing perspiration as a method of resolution. When fire excess prevails and dominates, moderate it with sour-cold; assist with bitter-sweet. Use acid to astringe, bitter to promote eruption, and acid again for restoration—this is the same as treating heat excess. When dryness excess prevails and dominates, use bitter-moist substances to regulate it; assist with sour-pungent ones. Employ bitter to purge. When cold excess prevails and dominates, modulate it with pungent-warm substances; assist them with sweet-bitter. Use salty to purge.

	至真要大... :	
帝曰：善。邪氣反勝，治之柰何。歧伯曰：風司于地，清反勝之，治以酸溫，佐以苦甘，以辛平之。熱司于地，寒反勝之，治以甘熱，佐以苦辛，以鹹平之。濕司于地，熱反勝之，治以苦冷，佐以鹹甘，以苦平之。火司于地，寒反勝之，治以甘熱，佐以苦辛，以鹹平之。燥司于地，熱反勝之，治以平寒，佐以苦甘，以酸平之，以和為利。寒司于地，熱反勝之，治以鹹冷，佐以甘辛，以苦平之。帝曰：其司天邪勝何如。歧伯曰：風化於天，清反勝之，治以酸溫，佐以甘苦。熱化於天，寒反勝之，治以甘溫，佐以苦酸辛。濕化於天，熱反勝之，治以苦寒，佐以苦酸。火化於天，寒反勝之，治以甘熱，佐以苦辛。燥化於天，熱反勝之，治以辛寒，佐以苦甘。寒化於天，熱反勝之，治以鹹冷，佐以苦辛。
		The Emperor said: "Good." What about when pathogenic qi unexpectedly prevails—how should one treat it? Qibo said: "When wind governs on earth, but clear (cold) qi unexpectedly overcomes it, treat with sour-warm substances; assist with bitter-sweet, and use pungent to balance." When heat governs on earth but cold unexpectedly overcomes it, treat it with sweet-warm substances; support with bitter-pungent, and use salty to balance. When damp governs on earth but heat unexpectedly overcomes it, treat with bitter-cold substances; assist with salty-sweet ones, and use bitter to balance. When fire governs on earth but cold unexpectedly prevails over it, treat with sweet-warm; assist with bitter-pungent; and use salty to regulate. When dryness governs on earth but heat prevails over it unexpectedly, treat with neutral-cold substances; assist with bitter-sweet; use sour to regulate, and harmony is beneficial. When cold governs on earth but heat overcomes it unexpectedly, treat with salty-cold; assist with sweet-pungent; use bitter to regulate. The Emperor asked: "What about when pathogenic forces overcome the governing qi of heaven?" Qibo said: "When wind transformation occurs in heaven, but clear (cold) unexpectedly overcomes it, treat with acid-warm substances; assist with sweet-bitter ones." When heat transformation occurs in heaven but cold unexpectedly overcomes it, treatment should be with sweet-warm substances, assisted by bitter-sour-pungent. When damp transformation occurs in heaven but heat unexpectedly overcomes it, use bitter-cold for treatment; assist with bitter-sour substances. When fire transformation occurs in heaven but cold prevails over it unexpectedly, use sweet-warm for treatment; assist with bitter-pungent substances. When dryness transformation occurs in heaven but heat prevails over it unexpectedly treat with pungent-cold, and assist with bitter-sweet. When cold transformation occurs in heaven but heat overcomes it unexpectedly treat with salty-cold substances, and assist with bitter-pungent.

顯示相似段落
	至真要大... :	
帝曰：六氣相勝柰何。歧伯曰：厥陰之勝，耳鳴頭眩，憒憒欲吐，胃鬲如寒，大風數舉，倮蟲不滋，胠脇氣并，化而為熱，小便黃赤，胃脘當心而痛，上支兩脇，腸鳴飱泄，少腹痛，注下赤白，甚則嘔吐，鬲咽不通。少陰之勝，心下熱善飢，齊下反動，氣遊三焦，炎暑至，木乃津，草乃萎，嘔逆躁煩，腹滿痛溏泄，傳為赤沃。太陰之勝，火氣內鬱，瘡瘍於中，流散於外，病在胠脇，甚則心痛熱格，頭痛喉痺項強，獨勝則濕氣內鬱，寒迫下焦，痛留頂，互引眉閒，胃滿，雨數至，燥化乃見，少腹滿腰脽重強，內不便善注泄，足下溫，頭重足脛胕腫，飲發於中，胕腫於上。少陽之勝，熱客於胃，煩心心痛，目赤欲嘔，嘔酸善飢，耳痛溺赤，善驚譫妄，暴熱消爍，草萎水涸，介蟲乃屈，少腹痛下沃赤白。陽明之勝，清發於中，左胠脇痛溏泄，內為嗌塞，外發㿉疝，大涼肅殺，華英改容，毛蟲乃殃，胸中不便，嗌塞而欬。太陽之勝，凝凓且至，非時水冰，羽乃後化，痔瘧發，寒厥入胃，則內生心痛，陰中乃瘍，隱曲不利，互引陰股，筋肉拘苛，血脈凝泣，絡滿色變，或為血泄，皮膚否腫，腹滿食減，熱反上行，頭項囟頂腦戶中痛，目如脫寒入下焦，傳為濡寫。帝曰：治之柰何。歧伯曰：厥陰之勝，治以甘清，佐以苦辛，以酸寫之。少陰之勝，治以辛寒，佐以苦鹹，以甘寫之。太陰之勝，治以鹹熱，佐以辛甘，以苦寫之。少陽之勝，治以辛寒，佐以甘鹹，以甘寫之。陽明之勝，治以酸溫，佐以辛甘，以苦泄之。太陽之勝，治以甘熱，佐以辛酸，以鹹寫之。
		The Emperor asked: "How should one deal with the mutual overcoming of the six qi?" Qibo said: "When Jueyin prevails, there will be ringing in the ears and dizziness of the head, confusion with a desire to vomit, a sensation of cold between the stomach and diaphragm. Frequent strong winds arise; hairless insects do not reproduce well. Qi gathers in the hypochondriac regions and transforms into heat. The urine becomes yellow or red. Pain occurs at the epigastrium, radiating upward to support both hypochondria. Intestinal rumbling and diarrhea with undigested food occur. There is pain in the lower abdomen, discharges of red and white mucus; in severe cases, vomiting occurs, and the throat and esophagus become obstructed." When Shaoyin prevails, heat accumulates beneath the heart with a strong desire to eat; there is movement below the navel. Qi circulates through the triple burner. Intense summer heat arises; trees become moistened while grasses wither. Nausea and vomiting occur along with restlessness and irritability. The abdomen becomes full, painful, and loose stools are passed; this may progress to bloody diarrhea. When Taiyin prevails, fire qi becomes internally stagnated; sores and ulcers arise within the body and spread outward. The disease manifests in the hypochondriac region; in severe cases it causes heart pain with heat obstruction, headache, throat numbness, and stiffness of the neck. If this condition dominates alone, dampness becomes internally stagnant, cold forces downward into the lower jiao, causing pain at the vertex of the head that radiates to the eyebrows. The stomach feels full. Frequent rains occur; dry transformation then appears. There is distention in the lower abdomen and heaviness and rigidity in the waist and hips. Bowel movements are difficult with frequent diarrhea. The feet feel warm while the head feels heavy, and swelling occurs in the legs and ankles. Fluids accumulate internally and swell upward. When Shaoyang prevails, heat lodges in the stomach; there is irritability of the heart and chest pain. The eyes become red with a desire to vomit; sour vomiting occurs along with a strong appetite. There is ear pain and reddish urine. One becomes easily startled and delirious. Sudden intense heat scorches away moisture, causing grasses to wither and water sources to dry up. Shelled creatures curl inward. Pain in the lower abdomen arises, accompanied by bloody or purulent discharge. When Yangming prevails, cold arises internally; there is pain in the left hypochondriac region and loose stools. Internally it causes obstruction of the throat; externally it manifests as carbuncles or hernias. Intense coolness brings severe killing effects, causing flowers and buds to wither; hairy creatures suffer misfortune. There is discomfort in the chest, obstruction of the throat, and coughing. When Taiyang prevails, severe cold suddenly arrives; water freezes even at inappropriate times. Birds delay their transformation. Hemorrhoids and malaria arise. Cold 厥 enters the stomach, causing internal heart pain. Ulcers form in the genital area. There is difficulty with urination, which radiates to the lower abdomen. Tendons and muscles become tense and rigid. Blood vessels congeal and stagnate; collateral channels fill up and change color. It may manifest as bloody diarrhea or edema of the skin. The abdomen becomes distended and appetite decreases. Heat unexpectedly rises upward, causing pain in the head, neck, fontanelle, vertex, and occipital region. The eyes feel as if they are about to fall out; cold invades the lower jiao and transforms into profuse diarrhea with watery stools. The Emperor asked: "How should these conditions be treated?" Qibo said: "When Jueyin prevails excessively, treat with sweet-cold substances; assist with bitter-pungent ones, and use sour to purge." When Shaoyin prevails excessively, treat it with pungent-cold substances; assist with bitter and salty ones, and use sweet to purge. When Taiyin prevails excessively, treatment should be with salty-warm substances; assist with pungent-sweet ones; and use bitter to purge. When Shaoyang prevails excessively, treat it using pungent-cold substances, assist with sweet-salty ones, and use sweet to eliminate. When Yangming prevails excessively, treat it by using sour-warm substances; assist with pungen-sweet ones, and employ bitter to drain. When Taiyang prevails excessively, treat it with sweet-warm; support with pungent-acid substances, and use salty to purge.
